kept TRIGGER teams_parent_required on teams reads rows of team_members through check_team_edge() calling resources_with() calling reached_with() calling containers_with() calling granted_resources() calling caller_grantees(), which FOREIGN KEY (organization_id, user_id) REFERENCES memberships on team_members changes on the server alone, so the replica decides on them as they were before
downgraded FOREIGN KEY at every connection that writes from always enforced to enforced under PRAGMA foreign_keys: SQLite enforces a foreign key only while the connection carries PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON, which is off by default and is connection state rather than database state. The script's setup part sets it, and every connection that writes to the replica has to run that part, or the delete PostgreSQL refuses will leave an orphan row with no error.
